      paint: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#FFFFFF" x="4" y="4" width="24" height="20" stroke="#000"/><rect fill="#FF0000" x="8" y="26" width="4" height="4"/><rect fill="#00FF00" x="14" y="26" width="4" height="4"/><rect fill="#0000FF" x="20" y="26" width="4" height="4"/><path fill="#808080" d="M20 8l8 12H12z"/></svg>`,
      help: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><circle fill="#FFFF00" cx="16" cy="16" r="12" stroke="#000" stroke-width="2"/><text x="16" y="22" text-anchor="middle" font-size="18" font-weight="bold" fill="#000">?</text></svg>`,
      run: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#C0C0C0" x="4" y="8" width="24" height="16" stroke="#000"/><path fill="#000" d="M12 12l8 4-8 4z"/></svg>`,
      shutdown: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#C0C0C0" x="6" y="4" width="20" height="24" stroke="#000"/><path fill="#FF0000" d="M14 8h4v10h-4z"/><circle fill="#FF0000" cx="16" cy="22" r="3"/></svg>`,
      windows: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#FF0000" x="2" y="2" width="12" height="12"/><rect fill="#00FF00" x="16" y="2" width="12" height="12"/><rect fill="#0000FF" x="2" y="16" width="12" height="12"/><rect fill="#FFFF00" x="16" y="16" width="12" height="12"/></svg>`,
      file: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#FFFFFF" x="8" y="2" width="16" height="28" stroke="#000"/><line stroke="#000" x1="12" y1="8" x2="20" y2="8"/><line stroke="#000" x1="12" y1="12" x2="20" y2="12"/><line stroke="#000" x1="12" y1="16" x2="20" y2="16"/></svg>`,
      drive: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#C0C0C0" x="2" y="10" width="28" height="14" stroke="#000"/><rect fill="#808080" x="4" y="12" width="8" height="10"/><rect fill="#000080" x="14" y="14" width="12" height="2"/><circle fill="#FF0000" cx="26" cy="17" r="2"/></svg>`,
      settings: `<svg viewBox="0 0 32 32"><rect fill="#C0C0C0" x="4" y="4" width="24" height="24" stroke="#000"/><circle fill="#000080" cx="16" cy="16" r="6"/><rect fill="#FFFF00" x="14" y="6" width="4" height="4"/><rect fill="#FFFF00" x="14" y="22" width="4" height="4"/><rect fill="#FFFF00" x="6" y="14" width="4" height="4"/><rect fill="#FFFF00" x="22" y="14" width="4" height="4"/></svg>`,
    };

    // ─── State ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let zIndexCounter = 100;
    let windowIdCounter = 0;
    const windows = new Map();
    let activeWindowId = null;
    let startMenuOpen = false;
    let selectedDesktopIcon = null;

    const desktopIcons = [
      { id: 'my-computer', label: 'My Computer', icon: 'computer', action: () => openExplorer('My Computer') },
      { id: 'recycle-bin', label: 'Recycle Bin', icon: 'recycle', action: () => openExplorer('Recycle Bin') },
      { id: 'network', label: 'Network\nNeighborhood', icon: 'network', action: () => openExplorer('Network Neighborhood') },
      { id: 'my-docs', label: 'My Documents', icon: 'folder', action: () => openExplorer('My Documents') },
    ];

    const startMenuItems = [
      { label: 'Programs', icon: 'folder', submenu: [
        { label: 'Accessories', icon: 'folder', submenu: [
          { label: 'Notepad', icon: 'notepad', action: () => openNotepad() },
          { label: 'Paint', icon: 'paint', action: () => openPaint() },
          { label: 'Calculator', icon: 'calc', action: () => openCalculator() },
          { label: 'Games', icon: 'folder', submenu: [
            { label: 'Minesweeper', icon: 'mine', action: () => openMinesweeper() },
          ]},
        ]},
        { label: 'Windows Explorer', icon: 'folderOpen', action: () => openExplorer('Windows Explorer') },
      ]},
      { label: 'Documents', icon: 'folder', action: () => openExplorer('My Documents') },
      { label: 'Settings', icon: 'settings', submenu: [
        { label: 'Control Panel', icon: 'settings', action: () => openControlPanel() },
      ]},
      { label: 'Find', icon: 'help', action: () => alert('Find: Feature coming soon to a browser near you!') },
      { label: 'Help', icon: 'help', action: () => openAbout() },
      { label: 'Run...', icon: 'run', action: () => openRunDialog() },
      { separator: true },
      { label: 'Shut Down...', icon: 'shutdown', action: () => shutdown() },
    ];

    // ─── Boot Sequence ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    function boot() {
      const bar = document.getElementById('boot-bar');
      const text = document.getElementById('boot-text');
      const messages = [
        'Starting Windows 95...',
        'HIMEM.SYS loaded...',
        'Loading kernel...',
        'Starting GUI...',
        'Welcome to Windows 95!',
      ];
      let progress = 0;
      let msgIndex = 0;

      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        progress += Math.random() * 15 + 5;
        if (progress >= 100) {
          progress = 100;
          clearInterval(interval);
          setTimeout(() => {
            document.getElementById('boot-screen').classList.add('hidden');
            initDesktop();
          }, 500);
        }
        bar.style.width = progress + '%';
        if (msgIndex < messages.length - 1 && progress > (msgIndex + 1) * 20) {
          msgIndex++;
          text.textContent = messages[msgIndex];
        }
      }, 200);
    }

    // ─── Desktop Init ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    function initDesktop() {
      const desktop = document.getElementById('desktop');
      desktopIcons.forEach((item, i) => {
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'desktop-icon';
        el.style.left = '16px';
        el.style.top = (16 + i * 80) + 'px';
        el.dataset.id = item.id;
        el.innerHTML = `<div class="icon-img">${ICONS[item.icon]}</div><span>${item.label.replace('\n', '<br>')}</span>`;
        el.addEventListener('dblclick', () => item.action());
        el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          selectDesktopIcon(el);
        });
        desktop.appendChild(el);
      });

      renderStartMenu();
      updateClock();
      setInterval(updateClock, 1000);

      document.getElementById('start-btn').addEventListener('click', toggleStartMenu);
      document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        if (!e.target.closest('#start-menu') && !e.target.closest('#start-btn')) {
          closeStartMenu();
        }
        if (!e.target.closest('#context-menu')) {
          closeContextMenu();
        }
        if (!e.target.closest('.desktop-icon')) {
          deselectDesktopIcons();
        }
      });

      desktop.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        showContextMenu(e.clientX, e.clientY, [
          { label: 'Arrange Icons', action: () => arrangeIcons() },
          { label: 'Refresh', action: () => {} },
          { separator: true },
          { label: 'Properties', action: () => openAbout() },
        ]);
      });
    }

    function selectDesktopIcon(el) {
      deselectDesktopIcons();
      el.classList.add('selected');
      selectedDesktopIcon = el;
    }

    function deselectDesktopIcons() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon.selected').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('selected'));
      selectedDesktopIcon = null;
    }

    function arrangeIcons() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach((el, i) => {
        el.style.top = (16 + i * 80) + 'px';
        el.style.left = '16px';
      });
    }

    // ─── Clock ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function updateClock() {
      const now = new Date();
      let h = now.getHours();
      const m = now.getMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0');
      const ampm = h >= 12 ? 'PM' : 'AM';
      h = h % 12 || 12;
      document.getElementById('clock').textContent = `${h}:${m} ${ampm}`;
    }

    // ─── Start Menu ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function renderStartMenu() {
      const container = document.getElementById('start-menu-items');
      container.innerHTML = '';
      startMenuItems.forEach(item => {
        if (item.separator) {
          const sep = document.createElement('div');
          sep.className = 'menu-separator';
          container.appendChild(sep);
          return;
        }
        container.appendChild(createMenuItem(item));
      });
    }

    function createMenuItem(item) {
      const el = document.createElement('div');
      el.className = 'menu-item';
      el.innerHTML = `<div class="menu-icon">${ICONS[item.icon]}</div><span>${item.label}</span>`;
      if (item.submenu) {
        el.innerHTML += '<span class="arrow">▶</span>';
        const sub = document.createElement('div');
        sub.className = 'submenu outset';
        item.submenu.forEach(subItem => {
          if (subItem.separator) {
            const sep = document.createElement('div');
            sep.className = 'menu-separator';
            sub.appendChild(sep);
          } else {
            sub.appendChild(createMenuItem(subItem));
          }
        });
        el.appendChild(sub);
      }
      el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (item.action) {
          closeStartMenu();
          item.action();
        }
      });
      return el;
    }

    function toggleStartMenu() {
      startMenuOpen = !startMenuOpen;
      document.getElementById('start-menu').classList.toggle('open', startMenuOpen);
      document.getElementById('start-btn').classList.toggle('active', startMenuOpen);
    }

    function closeStartMenu() {
      startMenuOpen = false;
      document.getElementById('start-menu').classList.remove('open');
      document.getElementById('start-btn').classList.remove('active');
    }

    // ─── Context Menu ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    function showContextMenu(x, y, items) {
      const menu = document.getElementById('context-menu');
      menu.innerHTML = '';
      items.forEach(item => {
        if (item.separator) {
          const sep = document.createElement('div');
          sep.className = 'menu-separator';
          menu.appendChild(sep);
          return;
        }
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.className = 'menu-item';
        el.textContent = item.label;
        el.addEventListener('click', () => { closeContextMenu(); item.action(); });
        menu.appendChild(el);
      });
      menu.style.left = x + 'px';
      menu.style.top = y + 'px';
      menu.classList.add('open');
    }

    function closeContextMenu() {
      document.getElementById('context-menu').classList.remove('open');
    }

    // ─── Window System ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    function createWindow({ title, icon, width = 400, height = 300, x, y, content, menuBar, statusBar, onClose, resizable = true }) {
      const id = ++windowIdCounter;
      x = x ?? 60 + (id * 24) % 200;
      y = y ?? 40 + (id * 20) % 150;

      const win = document.createElement('div');
      win.className = 'window outset';
      win.id = 'win-' + id;
      win.style.width = width + 'px';
      win.style.height = height + 'px';
      win.style.left = x + 'px';
      win.style.top = y + 'px';
      win.style.zIndex = ++zIndexCounter;

      let menuBarHTML = '';
      if (menuBar) {
        menuBarHTML = `<div class="menu-bar">${menuBar.map(m => `<span class="menu-bar-item">${m}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;
      }

      let statusBarHTML = '';
      if (statusBar) {
        statusBarHTML = `<div class="status-bar"><div class="status-panel inset">${statusBar}</div></div>`;
      }

      win.innerHTML = `
        <div class="title-bar">
          <div class="title-bar-text">${icon ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;width:16px;height:16px;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:4px">${ICONS[icon]}</span>` : ''}${title}</div>
          <div class="title-bar-controls">
            <button class="title-btn btn-outset minimize" title="Minimize">_</button>
            <button class="title-btn btn-outset maximize" title="Maximize">□</button>
            <button class="title-btn btn-outset close" title="Close">×</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        ${menuBarHTML}
        <div class="window-body">${content}</div>
        ${statusBarHTML}
      `;

      document.getElementById('windows').appendChild(win);

      const state = { id, el: win, title, icon, minimized: false, maximized: false, prevRect: null, onClose, resizable };
      windows.set(id, state);

      // Title bar drag
      const titleBar = win.querySelector('.title-bar');
      let dragging = false, dragX, dragY;

      titleBar.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        if (e.target.closest('.title-btn')) return;
        focusWindow(id);
        if (state.maximized) return;
        dragging = true;
        dragX = e.clientX - win.offsetLeft;
        dragY = e.clientY - win.offsetTop;
        e.preventDefault();
      });

      document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        if (!dragging) return;
        win.style.left = Math.max(0, e.clientX - dragX) + 'px';
        win.style.top = Math.max(0, e.clientY - dragY) + 'px';
      });

      document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { dragging = false; });

      titleBar.addEventListener('dblclick', () => toggleMaximize(id));

      win.querySelector('.minimize').addEventListener('click', () => minimizeWindow(id));
      win.querySelector('.maximize').addEventListener('click', () => toggleMaximize(id));
      win.querySelector('.close').addEventListener('click', () => closeWindow(id));

      win.addEventListener('mousedown', () => focusWindow(id));

      // Resize handle
      if (resizable) {
        const handle = document.createElement('div');
        handle.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;right:0;bottom:0;width:12px;height:12px;cursor:nwse-resize;';
        win.appendChild(handle);
        let resizing = false, startW, startH, startX, startY;
        handle.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
          if (state.maximized) return;
          e.stopPropagation();
          resizing = true;
          startW = win.offsetWidth;
          startH = win.offsetHeight;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startY = e.clientY;
          focusWindow(id);
        });
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
          if (!resizing) return;
          win.style.width = Math.max(200, startW + e.clientX - startX) + 'px';
          win.style.height = Math.max(120, startH + e.clientY - startY) + 'px';
        });
        document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { resizing = false; });
      }

      focusWindow(id);
      updateTaskbar();
      return { id, el: win, body: win.querySelector('.window-body') };
    }

    function focusWindow(id) {
      if (activeWindowId === id) return;
      windows.forEach((state, wid) => {
        state.el.classList.toggle('inactive', wid !== id);
        if (wid === id) {
          state.el.style.zIndex = ++zIndexCounter;
        }
      });
      activeWindowId = id;
      updateTaskbar();
    }

    function minimizeWindow(id) {
      const state = windows.get(id);
      if (!state) return;
      state.minimized = true;
      state.el.style.display = 'none';
      updateTaskbar();
    }

    function restoreWindow(id) {
      const state = windows.get(id);
      if (!state) return;
      state.minimized = false;
      state.el.style.display = 'flex';
      focusWindow(id);
    }

    function toggleMaximize(id) {
      const state = windows.get(id);
      if (!state) return;
      const win = state.el;
      if (state.maximized) {
        win.classList.remove('maximized');
        if (state.prevRect) {
          win.style.width = state.prevRect.w + 'px';
          win.style.height = state.prevRect.h + 'px';
          win.style.left = state.prevRect.x + 'px';
          win.style.top = state.prevRect.y + 'px';
        }
        state.maximized = false;
      } else {
        state.prevRect = { w: win.offsetWidth, h: win.offsetHeight, x: win.offsetLeft, y: win.offsetTop };
        win.classList.add('maximized');
        state.maximized = true;
      }
      updateTaskbar();
    }

    function closeWindow(id) {
      const state = windows.get(id);
      if (!state) return;
      if (state.onClose) state.onClose();
      state.el.remove();
      windows.delete(id);
      if (activeWindowId === id) {
        activeWindowId = null;
        const remaining = [...windows.keys()];
        if (remaining.length) focusWindow(remaining[remaining.length - 1]);
      }
      updateTaskbar();
    }

    function updateTaskbar() {
      const container = document.getElementById('task-buttons');
      container.innerHTML = '';
      windows.forEach((state, id) => {
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.className = 'task-btn btn-outset';
        if (id === activeWindowId && !state.minimized) btn.classList.add('active-window');
        btn.innerHTML = `${state.icon ? `<span class="task-icon">${ICONS[state.icon]}</span>` : ''}${state.title}`;
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (state.minimized) {
            restoreWindow(id);
          } else if (id === activeWindowId) {
            minimizeWindow(id);
          } else {
            focusWindow(id);
          }
        });
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });
    }

    // ─── Applications ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    function openNotepad() {
      createWindow({
        title: 'Untitled - Notepad',
        icon: 'notepad',
        width: 500,
        height: 350,
        menuBar: ['File', 'Edit', 'Search', 'Help'],
        content: `<textarea class="notepad-textarea" placeholder="Type here..."></textarea>`,
      });
    }

    function openCalculator() {
      const { body } = createWindow({
        title: 'Calculator',
        icon: 'calc',
        width: 240,
        height: 280,
        resizable: false,
        content: `<div class="calc-display inset" id="calc-display">0</div><div class="calc-grid" id="calc-grid"></div>`,
      });

      const display = body.querySelector('#calc-display');
      let current = '0', prev = null, op = null, fresh = true;

      const buttons = [
        'C', '±', '%', '÷',
        '7', '8', '9', '×',
        '4', '5', '6', '-',
        '1', '2', '3', '+',
        '0', '.', '=', '=',
      ];

      const grid = body.querySelector('#calc-grid');
      buttons.forEach((b, i) => {
        const btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.className = 'calc-btn btn-outset' + (b === '0' ? ' wide' : '');
        btn.textContent = b;
        if (b === '0') btn.style.gridColumn = 'span 2';
        if (i === buttons.length - 1) return;
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => handleCalc(b));
        grid.appendChild(btn);
      });

      function handleCalc(key) {
        if (key >= '0' && key <= '9') {
          current = fresh ? key : (current === '0' ? key : current + key);
          fresh = false;
        } else if (key === '.') {
          if (!current.includes('.')) current += '.';
          fresh = false;
        } else if (key === 'C') {
          current = '0'; prev = null; op = null; fresh = true;
        } else if (key === '±') {
          current = String(-parseFloat(current));
        } else if (key === '%') {
          current = String(parseFloat(current) / 100);
        } else if (['+', '-', '×', '÷'].includes(key)) {
          if (op && !fresh) compute();
          prev = parseFloat(current);
          op = key;
          fresh = true;
        } else if (key === '=') {
          compute();
          op = null;
          fresh = true;
        }
        display.textContent = current;
      }

      function compute() {
        if (prev === null || !op) return;
        const a = prev, b = parseFloat(current);
        let result;
        switch (op) {
          case '+': result = a + b; break;
          case '-': result = a - b; break;
          case '×': result = a * b; break;
          case '÷': result = b !== 0 ? a / b : 'Error'; break;
        }
        current = String(result);
        prev = null;
      }
    }

    function openExplorer(title = 'My Computer') {
      const drives = [
        { name: '3½ Floppy (A:)', icon: 'drive' },
        { name: '(C:) Local Disk', icon: 'drive' },
        { name: '(D:) CD-ROM', icon: 'drive' },
      ];
      const folders = [
        { name: 'Windows', icon: 'folder' },
        { name: 'Program Files', icon: 'folder' },
        { name: 'My Documents', icon: 'folder' },
        { name: 'AUTOEXEC.BAT', icon: 'file' },
        { name: 'CONFIG.SYS', icon: 'file' },
        { name: 'README.TXT', icon: 'file' },
      ];

      const isRecycle = title === 'Recycle Bin';
      const items = isRecycle ? [] : (title === 'My Computer' ? drives : folders);

      const { body } = createWindow({
        title,
        icon: title === 'My Computer' ? 'computer' : title === 'Recycle Bin' ? 'recycle' : 'folderOpen',
        width: 520,
        height: 380,
        menuBar: ['File', 'Edit', 'View', 'Help'],
        content: `
          <div class="explorer-layout">
            <div class="explorer-sidebar">
              <h4>All Folders</h4>
              <div class="explorer-tree-item">${ICONS.desktop || ICONS.computer}<span style="width:16px;display:inline-flex">${ICONS.computer}</span> Desktop</div>
              <div class="explorer-tree-item" style="padding-left:16px"><span style="width:16px;display:inline-flex">${ICONS.computer}</span> My Computer</div>
              <div class="explorer-tree-item" style="padding-left:32px"><span style="width:16px;display:inline-flex">${ICONS.drive}</span> (C:)</div>
              <div class="explorer-tree-item" style="padding-left:16px"><span style="width:16px;display:inline-flex">${ICONS.network}</span> Network</div>
            </div>
            <div style="flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column">
              <div class="explorer-toolbar">
                <button class="btn-outset" style="font-size:10px">Back</button>
                <button class="btn-outset" style="font-size:10px">Forward</button>
                <button class="btn-outset" style="font-size:10px">Up</button>
              </div>
              <div class="explorer-content">
                <div class="explorer-items" id="explorer-items"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `,
        statusBar: `${items.length} object(s)`,
      });

      const container = body.querySelector('#explorer-items');
      if (isRecycle) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="padding:16px;color:#808080">Recycle Bin is empty.</p>';
      } else {
        items.forEach(item => {
          const el = document.createElement('div');
          el.className = 'explorer-item';
          el.innerHTML = `${ICONS[item.icon]}<span>${item.name}</span>`;
          el.addEventListener('dblclick', () => {
            if (item.icon === 'folder') openExplorer(item.name);
            if (item.icon === 'file') openNotepad();
          });
          container.appendChild(el);
        });
      }
    }

    function openMinesweeper() {
      const ROWS = 9, COLS = 9, MINES = 10;
      const { body } = createWindow({
        title: 'Minesweeper',
        icon: 'mine',
        width: 200,
        height: 280,
        resizable: false,
        menuBar: ['Game', 'Help'],
        content: `<div class="mine-toolbar"><div class="mine-counter inset" id="mine-remaining">010</div><button class="mine-face btn-outset" id="mine-face">😊</button><div class="mine-counter inset" id="mine-timer">000</div></div><div class="mine-grid" id="mine-grid"></div>`,
      });

      let board, revealed, flagged, gameOver, timerInterval, seconds = 0;
      const grid = body.querySelector('#mine-grid');
      grid.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${COLS}, 16px)`;

      function initGame() {
        board = Array.from({ length: ROWS }, () => Array(COLS).fill(0));
        revealed = Array.from({ length: ROWS }, () => Array(COLS).fill(false));
        flagged = Array.from({ length: ROWS }, () => Array(COLS).fill(false));
        gameOver = false;
        seconds = 0;
        clearInterval(timerInterval);
        body.querySelector('#mine-timer').textContent = '000';
        body.querySelector('#mine-face').textContent = '😊';
        body.querySelector('#mine-remaining').textContent = String(MINES).padStart(3, '0');

        let placed = 0;
        while (placed < MINES) {
          const r = Math.floor(Math.random() * ROWS);
          const c = Math.floor(Math.random() * COLS);
          if (board[r][c] !== -1) { board[r][c] = -1; placed++; }
        }
        for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++)
          for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++)
            if (board[r][c] !== -1)
              board[r][c] = countAdjacent(r, c);

        renderGrid();
      }

      function countAdjacent(r, c) {
        let n = 0;
        for (let dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++)
          for (let dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
            const nr = r + dr, nc = c + dc;
            if (nr >= 0 && nr < ROWS && nc >= 0 && nc < COLS && board[nr][nc] === -1) n++;
          }
        return n;
      }

      function renderGrid() {
        grid.innerHTML = '';
        for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++)
          for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++) {
            const cell = document.createElement('div');
            cell.className = 'mine-cell';
            cell.dataset.r = r;
            cell.dataset.c = c;
            if (revealed[r][c]) {
              cell.classList.add('revealed');
              if (board[r][c] === -1) cell.textContent = '💣';
              else if (board[r][c] > 0) { cell.textContent = board[r][c]; cell.classList.add('n' + board[r][c]); }
            } else if (flagged[r][c]) {
              cell.textContent = '🚩';
            }
            cell.addEventListener('click', () => clickCell(r, c));
            cell.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); toggleFlag(r, c); });
            grid.appendChild(cell);
          }
      }

      function clickCell(r, c) {
        if (gameOver || flagged[r][c] || revealed[r][c]) return;
        if (seconds === 0) {
          timerInterval = setInterval(() => {
            seconds++;
            body.querySelector('#mine-timer').textContent = String(Math.min(seconds, 999)).padStart(3, '0');
          }, 1000);
        }
        revealed[r][c] = true;
        if (board[r][c] === -1) {
          gameOver = true;
          body.querySelector('#mine-face').textContent = '😵';
          clearInterval(timerInterval);
          for (let rr = 0; rr < ROWS; rr++)
            for (let cc = 0; cc < COLS; cc++)
              if (board[rr][cc] === -1) revealed[rr][cc] = true;
        } else if (board[r][c] === 0) {
          floodFill(r, c);
        }
        renderGrid();
        checkWin();
      }

      function floodFill(r, c) {
        for (let dr = -1; dr <= 1; dr++)
          for (let dc = -1; dc <= 1; dc++) {
            const nr = r + dr, nc = c + dc;
            if (nr >= 0 && nr < ROWS && nc >= 0 && nc < COLS && !revealed[nr][nc] && !flagged[nr][nc]) {
              revealed[nr][nc] = true;
              if (board[nr][nc] === 0) floodFill(nr, nc);
            }
          }
      }

      function toggleFlag(r, c) {
        if (gameOver || revealed[r][c]) return;
        flagged[r][c] = !flagged[r][c];
        const count = flagged.flat().filter(Boolean).length;
        body.querySelector('#mine-remaining').textContent = String(Math.max(0, MINES - count)).padStart(3, '0');
        renderGrid();
      }

      function checkWin() {
        let unrevealed = 0;
        for (let r = 0; r < ROWS; r++)
          for (let c = 0; c < COLS; c++)
            if (!revealed[r][c]) unrevealed++;
        if (unrevealed === MINES) {
          gameOver = true;
          body.querySelector('#mine-face').textContent = '😎';
          clearInterval(timerInterval);
        }
      }

      body.querySelector('#mine-face').addEventListener('click', initGame);
      initGame();
    }

    function openPaint() {
      const { body } = createWindow({
        title: 'untitled - Paint',
        icon: 'paint',
        width: 520,
        height: 420,
        menuBar: ['File', 'Edit', 'View', 'Image', 'Colors', 'Help'],
        content: `
          <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:100%">
            <div class="paint-toolbar">
              <button class="btn-outset paint-tool active" data-tool="pen">✏️</button>
              <button class="btn-outset paint-tool" data-tool="eraser">🧹</button>
              <button class="btn-outset paint-tool" data-tool="fill">🪣</button>
              <span style="width:8px"></span>
              ${['#000000','#808080','#800000','#808000','#008000','#008080','#000080','#800080','#FFFFFF','#C0C0C0','#FF0000','#FFFF00','#00FF00','#00FFFF','#0000FF','#FF00FF'].map((c,i) => `<div class="color-swatch${i===0?' active':''}" data-color="${c}" style="background:${c}"></div>`).join('')}
            </div>
            <div class="paint-canvas-wrap inset">
              <canvas id="paint-canvas" width="480" height="320"></canvas>
            </div>
          </div>
        `,
      });

      const canvas = body.querySelector('#paint-canvas');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      let drawing = false, tool = 'pen', color = '#000000', lastX, lastY;

      body.querySelectorAll('.paint-tool').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          body.querySelectorAll('.paint-tool').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
          btn.classList.add('active');
          tool = btn.dataset.tool;
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('.color-swatch').forEach(swatch => {
        swatch.addEventListener('click', () => {
          body.querySelectorAll('.color-swatch').forEach(s => s.classList.remove('active'));
          swatch.classList.add('active');
          color = swatch.dataset.color;
        });
      });

      canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
        drawing = true;
        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        lastX = e.clientX - rect.left;
        lastY = e.clientY - rect.top;
        if (tool === 'fill') {
          ctx.fillStyle = color;
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          drawing = false;
        }
      });

      canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
        if (!drawing) return;
        const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = e.clientX - rect.left;
        const y = e.clientY - rect.top;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(lastX, lastY);
        ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        ctx.strokeStyle = tool === 'eraser' ? '#FFFFFF' : color;
        ctx.lineWidth = tool === 'eraser' ? 16 : 2;
        ctx.lineCap = 'round';
        ctx.stroke();
        lastX = x;
        lastY = y;
      });

      canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { drawing = false; });
      canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { drawing = false; });
    }

    function openAbout() {
      createWindow({
        title: 'About Windows 95',
        icon: 'windows',
        width: 380,
        height: 260,
        resizable: false,
        content: `
          <div class="about-content">
            <div class="about-logo">${ICONS.windows}</div>
            <div class="about-text">
              <h2>Microsoft Windows 95</h2>
              <p>HTML Clone Edition</p>
              <p><br>Built with HTML, CSS & JavaScript</p>
              <p>© 1995-2026 Microsoft Corporation</p>
              <p>All rights reserved.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="dialog-buttons">
            <button class="btn-outset" id="about-ok">OK</button>
          </div>
        `,
      });
      setTimeout(() => {
        const okBtn = document.querySelector('#about-ok');
        if (okBtn) okBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          const win = this.closest('.window');
          const id = parseInt(win.id.replace('win-', ''));
          closeWindow(id);
        });
      }, 0);
    }

    function openControlPanel() {
      createWindow({
        title: 'Control Panel',
        icon: 'settings',
        width: 400,
        height: 300,
        content: `
          <div class="explorer-items" style="padding:16px">
            ${[
              { name: 'Display', icon: 'settings' },
              { name: 'Keyboard', icon: 'settings' },
              { name: 'Mouse', icon: 'settings' },
              { name: 'Sounds', icon: 'settings' },
              { name: 'Add/Remove Programs', icon: 'folder' },
              { name: 'Network', icon: 'network' },
            ].map(item => `<div class="explorer-item">${ICONS[item.icon]}<span>${item.name}</span></div>`).join('')}
          </div>
        `,
      });
    }

    function openRunDialog() {
      const { body, id } = createWindow({
        title: 'Run',
        icon: 'run',
        width: 340,
        height: 160,
        resizable: false,
        content: `
          <div style="padding:16px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px">
            <div style="width:32px">${ICONS.run}</div>
            <div style="flex:1">
              <p style="margin-bottom:8px">Type the name of a program, folder, document, or Internet resource, and Windows will open it for you.</p>
              <label style="display:block;margin-bottom:4px">Open:</label>
              <input type="text" id="run-input" class="inset" style="width:100%;padding:4px;font-family:inherit;font-size:11px" value="notepad">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="dialog-buttons">
            <button class="btn-outset" id="run-ok">OK</button>
            <button class="btn-outset" id="run-cancel">Cancel</button>
          </div>
        `,
      });

      body.querySelector('#run-ok').addEventListener('click', () => {
        const cmd = body.querySelector('#run-input').value.trim().toLowerCase();
        closeWindow(id);
        const apps = {
          notepad: openNotepad,
          calc: openCalculator,
          calculator: openCalculator,
          minesweeper: openMinesweeper,
          winmine: openMinesweeper,
          mspaint: openPaint,
          paint: openPaint,
          explorer: () => openExplorer('My Computer'),
        };
        if (apps[cmd]) apps[cmd]();
        else alert(`Windows cannot find '${cmd}'. Make sure you typed the name correctly, and then try again.`);
      });
      body.querySelector('#run-cancel').addEventListener('click', () => closeWindow(id));
    }

    function shutdown() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(w => w.remove());
      windows.clear();
      activeWindowId = null;
      updateTaskbar();
      document.getElementById('shutdown-screen').classList.add('open');
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById('shutdown-screen').classList.remove('open');
        document.getElementById('boot-screen').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('boot-bar').style.width = '0%';
        boot();
      }, 3000);
    }

    // ─── Start ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    boot();
  </script>
</body>

  .blink { animation: blink 1s steps(1) infinite; }
  @keyframes blink { 50% { opacity: 0; } }
</style>

<script>
"use strict";

/* ============================================================
   Window Manager
   ============================================================ */
const WM = {
  zTop: 100,
  windows: new Map(),
  active: null,
  seq: 0,
};

const desktop = document.getElementById('desktop');
const taskButtons = document.getElementById('task-buttons');

function bringToFront(win) {
  WM.zTop += 1;
  win.el.style.zIndex = WM.zTop;
  if (WM.active && WM.active !== win) {
    WM.active.el.classList.add('inactive');
    if (WM.active.taskBtn) WM.active.taskBtn.classList.remove('active');
  }
  win.el.classList.remove('inactive');
  if (win.taskBtn) win.taskBtn.classList.add('active');
  WM.active = win;
}

function makeWindow(opts) {
  const id = 'win' + (++WM.seq);
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'window outset';
  el.style.left = (opts.x ?? (60 + (WM.seq*22)%180)) + 'px';
  el.style.top = (opts.y ?? (40 + (WM.seq*22)%140)) + 'px';
  el.style.width = (opts.w ?? 360) + 'px';
  el.style.height = (opts.h ?? 260) + 'px';

  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="titlebar">
      <span class="tb-ico">${opts.icon || '📄'}</span>
      <span class="tb-title">${opts.title}</span>
      <span class="tb-btns">
        <span class="tb-btn" data-act="min">_</span>
        <span class="tb-btn" data-act="max">□</span>
        <span class="tb-btn" data-act="close">✕</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="window-body"></div>
    <div class="resizer"></div>
  `;
  desktop.appendChild(el);

  const win = {
    id, el, opts,
    body: el.querySelector('.window-body'),
    titlebar: el.querySelector('.titlebar'),
    minimized: false, maximized: false,
    prevRect: null,
    taskBtn: null,
  };
  WM.windows.set(id, win);

  // Title bar buttons
  el.querySelector('[data-act="close"]').addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); closeWindow(win); });
  el.querySelector('[data-act="min"]').addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); minimizeWindow(win); });
  el.querySelector('[data-act="max"]').addEventListener('click', e => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleMax(win); });
  win.titlebar.addEventListener('dblclick', () => toggleMax(win));

  el.addEventListener('mousedown', () => bringToFront(win));

  makeDraggable(win);
  makeResizable(win);

  // Taskbar button
  const tb = document.createElement('div');
  tb.className = 'task-btn outset';
  tb.innerHTML = `<span>${opts.icon||'📄'}</span><span class="t-label">${opts.title}</span>`;
  tb.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (win.minimized) { restoreWindow(win); bringToFront(win); }
    else if (WM.active === win) { minimizeWindow(win); }
    else { bringToFront(win); }
  });
  taskButtons.appendChild(tb);
  win.taskBtn = tb;

  bringToFront(win);
  if (opts.build) opts.build(win);
  return win;
}

function closeWindow(win) {
  if (win.opts.onClose) win.opts.onClose(win);
  win.el.remove();
  if (win.taskBtn) win.taskBtn.remove();
  WM.windows.delete(win.id);
  if (WM.active === win) WM.active = null;
}

function minimizeWindow(win) {
  win.minimized = true;
  win.el.style.display = 'none';
  win.taskBtn.classList.remove('active');
  if (WM.active === win) WM.active = null;
}
function restoreWindow(win) {
  win.minimized = false;
  win.el.style.display = 'flex';
}
function toggleMax(win) {
  if (win.maximized) {
    win.el.style.left = win.prevRect.left;
    win.el.style.top = win.prevRect.top;
    win.el.style.width = win.prevRect.width;
    win.el.style.height = win.prevRect.height;
    win.maximized = false;
  } else {
    win.prevRect = { left: win.el.style.left, top: win.el.style.top, width: win.el.style.width, height: win.el.style.height };
    win.el.style.left = '0px';
    win.el.style.top = '0px';
    win.el.style.width = desktop.clientWidth + 'px';
    win.el.style.height = desktop.clientHeight + 'px';
    win.maximized = true;
  }
}

function makeDraggable(win) {
  const tb = win.titlebar;
  let sx, sy, ox, oy, dragging = false;
  tb.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    if (e.target.closest('.tb-btn')) return;
    if (win.maximized) return;
    dragging = true;
    sx = e.clientX; sy = e.clientY;
    ox = win.el.offsetLeft; oy = win.el.offsetTop;
    bringToFront(win);
    e.preventDefault();
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (!dragging) return;
    let nx = ox + (e.clientX - sx);
    let ny = oy + (e.clientY - sy);
    ny = Math.max(0, ny);
    win.el.style.left = nx + 'px';
    win.el.style.top = ny + 'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => dragging = false);
}

function makeResizable(win) {
  const rz = win.el.querySelector('.resizer');
  let sx, sy, ow, oh, resizing = false;
  rz.addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
    resizing = true;
    sx = e.clientX; sy = e.clientY;
    ow = win.el.offsetWidth; oh = win.el.offsetHeight;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    bringToFront(win);
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
    if (!resizing) return;
    win.el.style.width = Math.max(160, ow + (e.clientX - sx)) + 'px';
    win.el.style.height = Math.max(80, oh + (e.clientY - sy)) + 'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => resizing = false);
}

/* ============================================================
   Virtual File System (in-memory)
   ============================================================ */
const FS = {
  readme: "Welcome to Windows 95 (HTML Edition)!\r\n\r\nThis is a loving recreation of the classic\r\ndesktop, built entirely in a single HTML file.\r\n\r\nThings to try:\r\n  * Double-click desktop icons\r\n  * Open the Start menu\r\n  * Play Minesweeper\r\n  * Draw something in Paint\r\n  * Drag and resize windows\r\n\r\nEnjoy the nostalgia!",
  todo: "TODO\r\n----\r\n[x] Boot up\r\n[x] Open Start menu\r\n[ ] Defrag the hard drive\r\n[ ] Find the Solitaire cards\r\n[ ] Upgrade to Windows 98",
};

   APP LAUNCHERS
   ============================================================ */
const Apps = {};

/* ---------- Notepad ---------- */
Apps.notepad = function(content, fname) {
  makeWindow({
    title: (fname || 'Untitled') + ' - Notepad', icon: '📝', w: 420, h: 300,
    build(win) {
      win.body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="menubar outset" style="border:none;box-shadow:none;">
          <span class="menu-item" data-m="file">File</span>
          <span class="menu-item" data-m="edit">Edit</span>
          <span class="menu-item" data-m="format">Format</span>
          <span class="menu-item" data-m="help">Help</span>
        </div>
        <textarea class="notepad wrap inset" spellcheck="false"></textarea>`;
      const ta = win.body.querySelector('textarea');
      ta.value = content || '';
      win.body.querySelector('[data-m="format"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
        ta.classList.toggle('wrap'); ta.classList.toggle('nowrap');
      });
      win.body.querySelector('[data-m="file"]').addEventListener('click', () => {
        Apps.notepad('', 'Untitled');
      });
      win.body.querySelector('[data-m="help"]').addEventListener('click', () => showAbout());
      win.body.querySelector('[data-m="edit"]').addEventListener('click', () => ta.focus());
    }
  });
};

/* ---------- Calculator ---------- */
Apps.calc = function() {
  makeWindow({
    title: 'Calculator', icon: '🧮', w: 220, h: 250,
    build(win) {
      win.body.innerHTML = `
        <input class="inset" id="calc-disp" readonly value="0"
          style="text-align:right;font-size:16px;padding:4px;margin:3px;font-family:'Lucida Console',monospace;">
        <div id="calc-keys" style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:3px;padding:3px;flex:1;"></div>`;
      const disp = win.body.querySelector('#calc-disp');
      const keys = win.body.querySelector('#calc-keys');
      let cur = '0', prev = null, op = null, reset = false;
      const upd = () => disp.value = cur;
      const layout = ['C','±','%','/','7','8','9','*','4','5','6','-','1','2','3','+','0','.','='];
      const spans = {'=':2};
      layout.forEach(k => {
        const b = document.createElement('button');
        b.className = 'btn95'; b.textContent = k; b.style.minWidth='0';
        if (k === '0') b.style.gridColumn = 'span 2';
        b.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (/[0-9]/.test(k)) {
            if (cur === '0' || reset) { cur = k; reset = false; } else cur += k;
          } else if (k === '.') { if (!cur.includes('.')) cur += '.'; }
          else if (k === 'C') { cur='0'; prev=null; op=null; }
          else if (k === '±') { cur = String(parseFloat(cur)*-1); }
          else if (k === '%') { cur = String(parseFloat(cur)/100); }
          else if (k === '=') {
            if (op && prev !== null) { cur = String(calc(parseFloat(prev), parseFloat(cur), op)); op=null; prev=null; reset=true; }
          } else { // operator
            if (op && prev !== null && !reset) { cur = String(calc(parseFloat(prev), parseFloat(cur), op)); }
            prev = cur; op = k; reset = true;
          }
          upd();
        });
        keys.appendChild(b);
      });
      function calc(a,b,o){ return o==='+'?a+b:o==='-'?a-b:o==='*'?a*b:o==='/'?(b?a/b:0):b; }
    }
  });
};

/* ---------- Clock ---------- */
Apps.clock = function() {
  makeWindow({
    title: 'Clock', icon: '🕐', w: 240, h: 160,
    build(win) {
      win.body.innerHTML = `<div class="clock-app inset"><div class="clock-time">--:--:--</div><div class="clock-date"></div></div>`;
      const t = win.body.querySelector('.clock-time');
      const d = win.body.querySelector('.clock-date');
      const tick = () => {
        const now = new Date();
        t.textContent = now.toLocaleTimeString();
        d.textContent = now.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {weekday:'long', year:'numeric', month:'long', day:'numeric'});
      };
      tick();
      const iv = setInterval(tick, 1000);
      win.opts.onClose = () => clearInterval(iv);
    }
  });
};

/* ---------- Minesweeper ---------- */
Apps.minesweeper = function() {
  const COLS=9, ROWS=9, MINES=10;
  makeWindow({
    title: 'Minesweeper', icon: '💣', w: 210, h: 280,
    build(win) {
      win.body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="ms-wrap outset">
          <div class="ms-head inset">
            <span class="ms-counter ms-mines">010</span>
            <button class="btn95 ms-face">🙂</button>
            <span class="ms-counter ms-time">000</span>
          </div>
          <div class="ms-grid inset"></div>
        </div>`;
      const grid = win.body.querySelector('.ms-grid');
      const face = win.body.querySelector('.ms-face');
      const mineDisp = win.body.querySelector('.ms-mines');
      const timeDisp = win.body.querySelector('.ms-time');
      grid.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${COLS}, 18px)`;
      let cells, started, dead, won, flags, timer, time;

      function reset() {
        cells = []; started=false; dead=false; won=false; flags=0; time=0;
        clearInterval(timer);
        timeDisp.textContent='000'; mineDisp.textContent=String(MINES).padStart(3,'0');
        face.textContent='🙂';
        grid.innerHTML='';
        for (let r=0;r<ROWS;r++) for (let c=0;c<COLS;c++){
          const cell={r,c,mine:false,rev:false,flag:false,n:0,el:document.createElement('div')};
          cell.el.className='ms-cell covered outset';
          cell.el.addEventListener('click',()=>reveal(cell));
          cell.el.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{e.preventDefault();flag(cell);});
          grid.appendChild(cell.el);
          cells.push(cell);
        }
      }
      const at=(r,c)=> (r>=0&&r<ROWS&&c>=0&&c<COLS)?cells[r*COLS+c]:null;
      const neigh=cell=>{const out=[];for(let dr=-1;dr<=1;dr++)for(let dc=-1;dc<=1;dc++){if(dr||dc){const n=at(cell.r+dr,cell.c+dc);if(n)out.push(n);}}return out;};

      function plant(safe){
        let placed=0;
        while(placed<MINES){
          const i=Math.floor(Math.random()*cells.length);
          const cell=cells[i];
          if(cell.mine||cell===safe||neigh(safe).includes(cell))continue;
          cell.mine=true;placed++;
        }
        cells.forEach(c=>{c.n=neigh(c).filter(n=>n.mine).length;});
      }
      function flag(cell){
        if(dead||won||cell.rev)return;
        cell.flag=!cell.flag;
        cell.el.textContent=cell.flag?'🚩':'';
        flags+=cell.flag?1:-1;
        mineDisp.textContent=String(Math.max(0,MINES-flags)).padStart(3,'0');
      }
      function reveal(cell){
        if(dead||won||cell.flag||cell.rev)return;
        if(!started){started=true;plant(cell);timer=setInterval(()=>{time++;timeDisp.textContent=String(Math.min(999,time)).padStart(3,'0');},1000);}
        cell.rev=true;
        cell.el.className='ms-cell revealed';
        if(cell.mine){cell.el.textContent='💣';cell.el.style.background='#f00';boom();return;}
        if(cell.n>0){cell.el.textContent=cell.n;cell.el.classList.add('ms-c'+cell.n);}
        else{neigh(cell).forEach(reveal);}
        checkWin();
      }
      function boom(){
        dead=true;face.textContent='😵';clearInterval(timer);
        cells.forEach(c=>{if(c.mine&&!c.rev){c.el.className='ms-cell revealed';c.el.textContent='💣';}});
      }
      function checkWin(){
        if(cells.filter(c=>!c.rev).every(c=>c.mine)){
          won=true;face.textContent='😎';clearInterval(timer);
          cells.forEach(c=>{if(c.mine&&!c.flag){c.flag=true;c.el.textContent='🚩';}});
        }
      }
      face.addEventListener('click',reset);
      reset();
      win.opts.onClose=()=>clearInterval(timer);
    }
  });
};

/* ---------- Paint ---------- */
Apps.paint = function() {
  makeWindow({
    title: 'untitled - Paint', icon: '🎨', w: 460, h: 360,
    build(win) {
      win.body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="paint-wrap">
          <div class="paint-tools outset"></div>
          <div class="paint-main">
            <div class="paint-canvas-wrap inset"><canvas id="paint-canvas" width="380" height="240"></canvas></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="palette outset"></div>`;
      const canvas = win.body.querySelector('#paint-canvas');
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
      let tool='pencil', color='#000000', size=2, drawing=false, lastX,lastY, startX,startY, snapshot;

      const tools=[['pencil','✏️'],['brush','🖌️'],['eraser','🧹'],['line','📏'],['rect','▭'],['fill','🪣']];
      const toolbox = win.body.querySelector('.paint-tools');
      tools.forEach(([t,ic])=>{
        const b=document.createElement('div');
        b.className='paint-tool outset'+(t===tool?' active':'');
        b.textContent=ic; b.title=t;
        b.addEventListener('click',()=>{tool=t;toolbox.querySelectorAll('.paint-tool').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));b.classList.add('active');});
        toolbox.appendChild(b);
      });

      const colors=['#000000','#808080','#800000','#808000','#008000','#008080','#000080','#800080',
                    '#ffffff','#c0c0c0','#ff0000','#ffff00','#00ff00','#00ffff','#0000ff','#ff00ff'];
      const pal = win.body.querySelector('.palette');
      colors.forEach(c=>{
        const s=document.createElement('div');s.className='swatch';s.style.background=c;
        s.addEventListener('click',()=>color=c);
        pal.appendChild(s);
      });

      const pos=e=>{const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();return {x:Math.round((e.clientX-r.left)*(canvas.width/r.width)),y:Math.round((e.clientY-r.top)*(canvas.height/r.height))};};

      canvas.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
        const p=pos(e); drawing=true; startX=lastX=p.x; startY=lastY=p.y;
        if(tool==='fill'){floodFill(p.x,p.y,color);drawing=false;return;}
        if(tool==='line'||tool==='rect'){snapshot=ctx.getImageData(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);}
        if(tool==='pencil'||tool==='brush'||tool==='eraser'){dot(p.x,p.y);}
      });
      canvas.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
        if(!drawing)return;const p=pos(e);
        if(tool==='line'){ctx.putImageData(snapshot,0,0);stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(startX,startY);ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.stroke();}
        else if(tool==='rect'){ctx.putImageData(snapshot,0,0);stroke();ctx.strokeRect(startX,startY,p.x-startX,p.y-startY);}
        else if(tool==='pencil'||tool==='brush'||tool==='eraser'){stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(lastX,lastY);ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.stroke();lastX=p.x;lastY=p.y;}
      });
      document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>drawing=false);

      function stroke(){
        ctx.strokeStyle=tool==='eraser'?'#fff':color;
        ctx.fillStyle=tool==='eraser'?'#fff':color;
        ctx.lineWidth=tool==='brush'?6:tool==='eraser'?12:2;
        ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
      }
      function dot(x,y){stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,ctx.lineWidth/2,0,7);ctx.fill();}
      function floodFill(x,y,fill){
        const img=ctx.getImageData(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);const d=img.data;
        const W=canvas.width;const idx=(x,y)=>(y*W+x)*4;
        const t=idx(x,y);const tc=[d[t],d[t+1],d[t+2],d[t+3]];
        const fc=hexRGB(fill);
        if(tc[0]===fc[0]&&tc[1]===fc[1]&&tc[2]===fc[2])return;
        const stack=[[x,y]];
        while(stack.length){
          const [cx,cy]=stack.pop();
          if(cx<0||cy<0||cx>=W||cy>=canvas.height)continue;
          const i=idx(cx,cy);
          if(d[i]===tc[0]&&d[i+1]===tc[1]&&d[i+2]===tc[2]&&d[i+3]===tc[3]){
            d[i]=fc[0];d[i+1]=fc[1];d[i+2]=fc[2];d[i+3]=255;
            stack.push([cx+1,cy],[cx-1,cy],[cx,cy+1],[cx,cy-1]);
          }
        }
        ctx.putImageData(img,0,0);
      }
      function hexRGB(h){return [parseInt(h.slice(1,3),16),parseInt(h.slice(3,5),16),parseInt(h.slice(5,7),16)];}
    }
  });
};

  makeWindow({
    title: 'My Computer', icon: '🖥️', w: 420, h: 300,
    build(win) {

        <div class="explorer">
          <div class="icon-grid inset"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="statusbar">
          <span class="panel inset">5 object(s)</span>
          <span class="panel inset" style="flex:0 0 90px;">My Computer</span>
        </div>`;
      const grid = win.body.querySelector('.icon-grid');
      const items = [
        {ico:'💾',label:'3½ Floppy (A:)'},
        {ico:'🖴',label:'(C:)'},
        {ico:'💿',label:'(D:)'},
        {ico:'🖨️',label:'Printers'},

        grid.appendChild(fi);
      });
    }
  });
};

/* ---------- Help ---------- */
Apps.help = function() {
  makeWindow({
    title: 'Help', icon: '❓', w: 400, h: 320,
    build(win){
      win.body.innerHTML = `<div class="help-content inset">
        <h2>Windows 95 Help</h2>
        <p>Welcome to the HTML recreation of Windows 95. Here's how to get around:</p>
        <ul>
          <li><b>Start menu</b> — click <code>Start</code> for programs.</li>
          <li><b>Desktop icons</b> — double-click to open.</li>
          <li><b>Windows</b> — drag the title bar to move, drag the bottom-right corner to resize.</li>
          <li><b>Minimize / Maximize / Close</b> — use the buttons in each title bar.</li>
          <li><b>Taskbar</b> — click a button to switch or minimize a window.</li>
        </ul>
        <p><b>Included programs:</b> Notepad, Paint, Minesweeper, Calculator, Clock, My Computer.</p>
        <p>Right-click cells in Minesweeper to place flags. Have fun!</p>
      </div>`;
    }
  });
};

/* ---------- Run dialog ---------- */
Apps.run = function() {
  makeWindow({
    title: 'Run', icon: '🏃', w: 340, h: 150,
    build(win){
      win.body.innerHTML = `
        <div style="padding:12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;">
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;">
            <span style="font-size:30px;">🏃</span>
            <span style="line-height:1.4;">Type the name of a program, and Windows will open it for you.</span>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;">
            <span>Open:</span>
            <input class="inset" id="run-input" style="flex:1;padding:3px;font-family:inherit;" autocomplete="off">
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:6px;">
            <button class="btn95" id="run-ok">OK</button>
            <button class="btn95" id="run-cancel">Cancel</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
      const inp = win.body.querySelector('#run-input');
      inp.focus();
      const go = () => {
        const v = inp.value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\.exe$/,'');
        const map = {notepad:'notepad',paint:'paint',mspaint:'paint',calc:'calc',clock:'clock',
                     minesweeper:'minesweeper',winmine:'minesweeper',explorer:'explorer'};
        if (map[v]) { Apps[map[v]](); closeWindow(win); }
        else dialog('Run','⚠️',`Cannot find '${inp.value}'. Make sure you typed the name correctly.\n\nTry: notepad, paint, calc, clock, winmine, explorer`);
      };
      win.body.querySelector('#run-ok').addEventListener('click',go);
      win.body.querySelector('#run-cancel').addEventListener('click',()=>closeWindow(win));
      inp.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')go();});
    }
  });
};

Apps.settings = function(){ Apps.explorer(); };
Apps.find = function(){ dialog('Find','🔍','Find: All Files\n\nSearch is not available in this demo.'); };

/* ---------- Shutdown ---------- */
Apps.shutdown = function() {
  const overlay = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.style.cssText='position:absolute;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5);z-index:200000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;';
  const box = document.createElement('div');
  box.className='window outset';
  box.style.width='320px';
  box.innerHTML=`
    <div class="titlebar"><span class="tb-ico">🔌</span><span class="tb-title">Shut Down Windows</span></div>
    <div style="padding:16px;display:flex;gap:14px;">
      <span style="font-size:40px;">🔌</span>
      <div>
        <p style="margin-bottom:10px;font-weight:bold;">Are you sure you want to shut down the computer?</p>
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;">
          <label><input type="radio" name="sd" checked> Shut down</label>
          <label><input type="radio" name="sd"> Restart</label>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="dialog-btns">
      <button class="btn95" id="sd-yes">Yes</button>
      <button class="btn95" id="sd-no">No</button>
    </div>`;
  overlay.appendChild(box);
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  box.querySelector('#sd-no').addEventListener('click',()=>overlay.remove());
  box.querySelector('#sd-yes').addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const mode = box.querySelector('input[name=sd]:checked').parentElement.textContent.trim();
    overlay.remove();
    if (mode==='Restart'){ doShutdownScreen(true); }
    else doShutdownScreen(false);
  });
};

function doShutdownScreen(restart){
  const s=document.createElement('div');
  s.style.cssText='position:absolute;inset:0;background:#000;z-index:300000;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#fa3;font-size:22px;text-align:center;font-family:sans-serif;';
  s.innerHTML = restart
    ? 'Restarting...'
    : `<div>It's now safe to turn off<br>your computer.<br><br><span style="font-size:13px;color:#888;cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;" id="reboot">(click to restart)</span></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(s);
  if(restart){ setTimeout(()=>location.reload(),1200); }
  else { s.querySelector('#reboot').addEventListener('click',()=>location.reload()); }
}

/* ---------- Generic dialog ---------- */
function dialog(title, icon, msg) {
  makeWindow({
    title, icon, w: 320, h: 150, x: 200, y: 150,
    build(win){
      win.el.style.height='auto';
      win.body.innerHTML=`
        <div class="dialog-body"><span class="d-ico">${icon}</span><span style="white-space:pre-wrap;line-height:1.4;align-self:center;">${msg}</span></div>
        <div class="dialog-btns"><button class="btn95">OK</button></div>`;
      win.body.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click',()=>closeWindow(win));
      win.el.querySelector('.resizer').style.display='none';
    }
  });
}
function showAbout(){ dialog('About','ℹ️','Windows 95\nHTML Edition\n\nA single-file recreation.\nBuilt with HTML, CSS & JavaScript.'); }

/* ============================================================
   Desktop Icons
   ============================================================ */
const desktopIcons = [
  {ico:'🖥️', label:'My Computer', action:()=>Apps.explorer()},
  {ico:'📁', label:'My Documents', action:()=>Apps.explorer()},
  {ico:'🗑️', label:'Recycle Bin', action:()=>dialog('Recycle Bin','🗑️','The Recycle Bin is empty.')},
  {ico:'📝', label:'Notepad', action:()=>Apps.notepad('', 'Untitled')},
  {ico:'🎨', label:'Paint', action:()=>Apps.paint()},
  {ico:'💣', label:'Minesweeper', action:()=>Apps.minesweeper()},
  {ico:'🧮', label:'Calculator', action:()=>Apps.calc()},
  {ico:'🌐', label:'Internet', action:()=>dialog('The Internet','🌐','Dialing up...\n\nNo modem detected. The Internet will have to wait.')},
];

function layoutIcons(){
  desktop.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach(e=>e.remove());
  desktopIcons.forEach((it,i)=>{
    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.className='desktop-icon';
    el.style.left='10px';
    el.style.top=(10 + i*78)+'px';
    el.innerHTML=`<span class="ico">${it.ico}</span><br><span class="label">${it.label}</span>`;
    el.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      e.stopPropagation();
      desktop.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('selected'));
      el.classList.add('selected');
    });
    el.addEventListener('dblclick',it.action);
    makeIconDraggable(el);
    desktop.appendChild(el);
  });
}
function makeIconDraggable(el){
  let sx,sy,ox,oy,moving=false,moved=false;
  el.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
    moving=true;moved=false;sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;ox=el.offsetLeft;oy=el.offsetTop;
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
    if(!moving)return;
    if(Math.abs(e.clientX-sx)>3||Math.abs(e.clientY-sy)>3)moved=true;
    el.style.left=(ox+e.clientX-sx)+'px';
    el.style.top=Math.max(0,oy+e.clientY-sy)+'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>moving=false);
}

/* ============================================================
   Start menu logic
   ============================================================ */
const startBtn = document.getElementById('start-btn');
const startMenu = document.getElementById('start-menu');
let menuOpen = false;

function openStart(){ menuOpen=true; startMenu.classList.add('open'); startBtn.classList.add('active'); }
function closeStart(){ menuOpen=false; startMenu.classList.remove('open'); startBtn.classList.remove('active'); closeSubs(); }
function closeSubs(){ document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('open')); }

startBtn.addEventListener('click',e=>{ e.stopPropagation(); menuOpen?closeStart():openStart(); });

document.querySelectorAll('#start-list .start-entry').forEach(en=>{
  en.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>{
    closeSubs();
    const sub=en.dataset.sub;
    if(sub){
      const m=document.getElementById('sub-'+sub);
      const r=en.getBoundingClientRect();
      m.style.left=(startMenu.offsetLeft+startMenu.offsetWidth)+'px';
      m.style.bottom=(window.innerHeight - r.bottom)+'px';
      m.classList.add('open');
    }
  });
  en.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    const app=en.dataset.app;
    if(app){
      if(Apps[app])Apps[app]();
      closeStart();
    }
  });
});

document.querySelectorAll('.submenu .start-entry').forEach(en=>{
  en.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(en.dataset.app && Apps[en.dataset.app]) Apps[en.dataset.app]();
    if(en.dataset.doc){
      const d=en.dataset.doc;
      Apps.notepad(FS[d], d==='readme'?'Readme.txt':'Todo.txt');
    }
    closeStart();
  });
});

// keep submenu open when hovering it
document.querySelectorAll('.submenu').forEach(m=>{
  m.addEventListener('mouseenter',()=>m.classList.add('open'));
});

document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  if(menuOpen && !e.target.closest('#start-menu') && !e.target.closest('#start-btn') && !e.target.closest('.submenu'))
    closeStart();
  // deselect desktop icons when clicking empty desktop
  if(e.target===desktop) desktop.querySelectorAll('.desktop-icon').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('selected'));
});

/* ============================================================
   Rubber-band selection on desktop
   ============================================================ */
const selBox = document.getElementById('select-box');
(function(){
  let sx,sy,active=false;
  desktop.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
    if(e.target!==desktop)return;
    active=true;sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;
    selBox.style.display='block';
    selBox.style.left=sx+'px';selBox.style.top=sy+'px';selBox.style.width='0';selBox.style.height='0';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
    if(!active)return;
    const x=Math.min(e.clientX,sx),y=Math.min(e.clientY,sy);
    selBox.style.left=x+'px';selBox.style.top=y+'px';
    selBox.style.width=Math.abs(e.clientX-sx)+'px';selBox.style.height=Math.abs(e.clientY-sy)+'px';
  });
  document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>{active=false;selBox.style.display='none';});
})();

/* ============================================================
   Clock in tray
   ============================================================ */
function updateClock(){
  const now=new Date();
  let h=now.getHours();const m=String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
  const ap=h>=12?'PM':'AM';h=h%12||12;
  document.getElementById('clock').textContent=`${h}:${m} ${ap}`;
}
updateClock();
setInterval(updateClock,1000);

document.getElementById('clock').addEventListener('dblclick',()=>Apps.clock());
document.getElementById('tray-vol').addEventListener('click',()=>dialog('Volume','🔊','Volume control is not available in this demo.'));

/* ============================================================
   Boot
   ============================================================ */
layoutIcons();

// Welcome window
window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
  setTimeout(()=>Apps.notepad(FS.readme,'Readme.txt'),300);
});

// Prevent context menu on the whole desktop (except where we handle it)
document.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
  if(!e.target.closest('.ms-cell') && !e.target.closest('textarea') && !e.target.closest('input')) e.preventDefault();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

      box-shadow: none;
      background: #c0c0c0;
    }

    .about-logo {
      width: 64px;
      height: 64px;
      margin-right: 12px;
      float: left;
      display: grid;
      place-items: center;
      font-size: 36px;
      background: var(--blue);
      color: white;
      border: 2px inset var(--gray);
    }

    @media (max-width: 600px) {
      .icon { width: 68px; }
      .window {
        min-width: 190px;
        max-width: calc(100vw - 8px);
      }
      .task-button {
        min-width: 78px;
      }
      .start-menu {
        width: min(220px, calc(100vw - 4px));
      }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main class="desktop" id="desktop" aria-label="Windows 95 desktop">

    <div class="icon" tabindex="0" style="left:96px;top:94px" data-open="minesweeper">

    </div>
  </main>

  <nav class="start-menu" id="startMenu" aria-label="Start menu">
    <div class="start-stripe">Windows 95</div>
    <div class="start-items">
      <div class="start-item" data-open="computer"><span class="mini">▣</span>Programs</div>
      <div class="start-item" data-open="documents"><span class="mini">▤</span>Documents</div>
      <div class="start-item" data-open="settings"><span class="mini">⚙</span>Settings</div>

      <div class="start-item" data-open="run"><span class="mini">▶</span>Run...</div>
      <div class="divider"></div>
      <div class="start-item" data-open="notepad"><span class="mini">✎</span>Notepad</div>

      <div class="start-item" data-open="minesweeper"><span class="mini">✹</span>Minesweeper</div>

    </div>
